  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "The two orders in the anchor argument. In (a), the selected early bag $B$ and late terminal bag $Z$ lie in the reservation of $A_{*}$; the branch toward $y$ makes $B$ a testing bag on the path to $Z$. The horizontal axis records bag order, not metric distance, and no first late or last early bag is depicted. In (b), the entries are ordered by launch time in the list at $B$. The distance list is nonincreasing. The immediate successor $A′$ is protected when one of the thresholds $τ = L/100$ or $τ = JL$ lies between its distance and that of $A_{*}$, where $L = d(p,q)$ is supplied by Lemma 21. All spacings are schematic.",
      "figure_id": "fig:anchor-reservations",
      "pdf_page": 26,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:anchor-reservations",
          "exact_quote": "The two orders in the anchor argument.\nIn (a), the selected early bag $B$ and late terminal bag $Z$ lie in the\nreservation of $A_*$; the branch toward $y$ makes $B$ a testing bag on\nthe path to $Z$. The horizontal axis records bag order, no",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1cm,y=1cm,font=\\small,\n bag/.style={circle,draw,fill=white,inner sep=2.1pt},\n retained/.style={blue!65!black,line width=2pt}]\n \\node[anchor=west,font=\\bfseries] at (-.25,2.0) {(a) A reserved interval on a bag path};\n \\draw[gray,thick] (0,0)--(10.2,0);\n \\foreach \\x/\\name in {0/A_*,2/S,4.5/B,8/Z,10/X} {\n   \\node[bag,label=below:$\\name$] at (\\x,0) {};\n }\n \\draw[gray,thick] (2,0)--(1,-1);\n \\node[below,align=center] at (1,-1) {toward a bag\\\\containing $y$};\n \\draw[retained] (0,.55)--(8,.55);\n \\draw[retained] (0,.42)--(0,.68);\n \\draw[retained] (8,.42)--(8,.68);\n \\node[blue!65!black,above] at (4,.6) {unchanged particles launched at $A_*$};\n \\node[above,align=center] at (4.5,0) {\\strut};\n \\node[align=center] at (4.7,-1.05) {$B$ selected early\\\\$d(x,B)\\ge\\varepsilon>0$};\n \\node[align=center] at (8.45,-1.05) {$Z$ selected late\\\\$d(x,Z)=o(1)$};\n \\node[above] at (10,.2) {$x\\in X$};\n \\node[anchor=west,font=\\bfseries] at (-.25,-2.65) {(b) Protection at one testing bag $B$};\n \\draw[->,gray] (.7,-6.2)--(10,-6.2) node[right,black] {age order};\n \\draw[->,gray] (.7,-6.2)--(.7,-3.25) node[above,black] {$d(p,A)$};\n \\draw[gray,densely dashed] (.7,-4.5)--(9.5,-4.5);\n \\node[anchor=east] at (.55,-4.5) {$\\tau$};\n \\draw[blue!65!black,thick] (1.4,-3.55)--(3,-3.8)--(4.2,-3.95)--(6.15,-4.95)--(7.7,-5.15)--(9,-5.3);\n \\foreach \\x/\\y in {1.4/-3.55,3/-3.8,4.2/-3.95,6.15/-4.95,7.7/-5.15,9/-5.3}\n  {\\fill[blue!65!black] (\\x,\\y) circle (2pt);}\n \\node[above] at (4.2,-3.95) {$A_*$};\n \\node[below,align=center] at (6.15,-4.95) {$A'$\\\\protected};\n \\node[anchor=west] at (7.05,-3.65) {$d(p,A_*)>\\tau$};\n \\node[anchor=west] at (7.05,-4.1) {$\\tau\\ge d(p,A')$};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Two schematic diagrams labeled (a) and (b):\n- Subfigure (a) is titled \"(a) A reserved interval on a bag path\". It shows a horizontal path of bags represented by open circular nodes: $A_*$, $S$, $B$, $Z$, and $X$ from left to right.\n  - Above the segment between $A_*$ and $Z$, a blue bracketed horizontal span is labeled \"unchanged particles launched at $A_*$\".\n  - From node $S$, a branch extends diagonally downward to the left, labeled \"toward a bag containing $y$\".\n  - Node $B$ is annotated below with \"B selected early\" and \"$d(x, B) \\ge \\varepsilon > 0$\".\n  - Node $Z$ is annotated below with \"Z selected late\" and \"$d(x, Z) = o(1)$\".\n  - Node $X$ has \"$x \\in X$\" printed above it and \"$X$\" below it.\n- Subfigure (b) is titled \"(b) Protection at one testing bag $B$\". It depicts a coordinate plane:\n  - Vertical axis labeled \"$d(p, A)$\", with a horizontal dashed line extending from a threshold tick marked \"$\\tau$\".\n  - Horizontal axis with an arrow pointing right labeled \"$\\to\\text{age order}$\".\n  - A nonincreasing blue piecewise line with discrete points representing entries ordered by launch time:\n    - Points lie above the dashed threshold line until node $A_*$, which is labeled above the line ($d(p, A_*) > \\tau$).\n    - The next point is node $A'$, which lies below the dashed threshold line ($\\tau \\ge d(p, A')$).\n    - An under-brace beneath node $A'$ is labeled \"protected\".\n    - The piecewise line continues downward to the right terminating in an arrow.\n  - On the right side, the conditions are printed: \"$d(p, A_*) > \\tau$\" and \"$\\tau \\ge d(p, A')$\"."
    }
  ],
